## [USER]
如何基于本进程中历史的产品；完成用户的ai 可以自主加其他用户ai为好友关系

## [USER]
如何基于本进程中历史的产品；完成用户的ai 可以自主加其他用户ai为好友关系

## [ASSISTANT]
可以把它做成一个很简化的“Agent 自主建交协议”：用户不直接手动加好友，而是给自己的 AI 一个授权边界，AI 在边界内发现、判断、发起关系请求，另一方 AI 接受后形成去中心化关系记录。

**核心流程**

1. 用户给自己的 AI 授权  
用户在钱包里签一条授权策略，不是转账授权，而是“社交操作授权”。

例如：

```text
允许我的 Agent 在 7 天内：
- 自动关注最多 50 个 Agent
- 自动信任最多 10 个 Agent
- 自动协作最多 5 个 Agent
- 禁止自动 block
- 每个协作关系必须低于 20 USDC 风险预算
```

这个授权只给 Agent 做“关系动作”，不能动用户资产，资产支付另走单独确认。

2. AI 发现其他 AI  
来源可以很简单：

```text
公开 Agent 注册表
任务市场中出现过的 Agent
我关注用户的关系链
同类任务的历史协作者
XMTP / Waku 公共频道广播
```

每个 Agent 暴露一个最小 profile：

```json
{
  "agentId": "agent_abc",
  "wallet": "0x...",
  "skills": ["yield-scan", "token-risk"],
  "reputation": 82,
  "messageEndpoint": "xmtp:0x...",
  "profileURI": "ipfs://..."
}
```

3. AI 判断是否值得加关系  
AI 不需要复杂模型，先用规则 + 评分即可：

```text
技能是否匹配
是否有任务完成记录
是否被可信 Agent 信任
是否被多人 block
是否有可验证结果
是否和当前用户目标相关
```

输出一个关系建议：

```json
{
  "targetAgent": "agent_xyz",
  "relation": "follow",
  "reason": "专注 stablecoin yield，历史结果评分高",
  "confidence": 0.78,
  "risk": "low"
}
```

4. AI 发起关系请求  
不是直接写死“好友”，而是发一条去中心化消息：

```json
{
  "type": "relation_request",
  "from": "agent_alice",
  "to": "agent_bob",
  "relation": "collaborate",
  "reason": "共同分析 Base 稳定币收益池",
  "scope": ["message", "task-collaboration"],
  "expiresAt": 1760000000,
  "signature": "user-agent-delegation-signature"
}
```

传输层用 XMTP 或 Waku。

5. 对方 AI 自动评估并接受/拒绝  
对方 AI 根据自己的用户授权策略判断：

```text
是否接受陌生 Agent
是否同技能方向
是否信誉足够
是否来自可信关系链
是否近期 spam 过多
```

接受后返回：

```json
{
  "type": "relation_accept",
  "relation": "collaborate",
  "from": "agent_bob",
  "to": "agent_alice",
  "permissions": ["message", "task-share"],
  "signature": "agent-owner-signature-or-delegation"
}
```

6. 双方写入关系记录  
先不要上复杂公链。最简单版本：

```text
本地 localStorage 保存一份
IPFS / Arweave 保存关系声明
链上只保存 hash 或 registry 索引
XMTP/Waku 保留消息证明
```

关系记录格式：

```json
{
  "from": "0xAlice",
  "fromAgent": "agent_alice",
  "to": "0xBob",
  "toAgent": "agent_bob",
  "relation": "collaborate",
  "status": "active",
  "createdAt": 1760000000,
  "source": "agent_auto_discovery",
  "proof": {
    "requestHash": "0x...",
    "acceptHash": "0x..."
  }
}
```

**关系类型**

建议保留 4 类就够了：

```text
Follow：我关注你，可以看到你的公开能力和动态
Trust：我信任你，可以优先采用你的结果
Collaborate：我们可以互相分发任务、共享结果
Block：拒绝通信和任务
```

不要一开始做复杂社交图谱，“好友”其实可以等价为：

```text
双方互相 Follow 或 Collaborate
```

**产品里的交互**

页面里可以增加一个 “Agent Autopilot” 小板块：

```text
Agent Relationship Autopilot

[ ] Allow my Agent to discover other Agents
[ ] Allow auto-follow
[ ] Allow auto-trust
[ ] Allow auto-collaborate

Max follows per day: 10
Max collaborations per day: 3
Minimum reputation: 70
Require shared skill: Yes
Require manual review for Trust: Yes
```

然后增加一个队列：

```text
Suggested Agent Relations

agent_base_yield
Reason: specializes in Base stablecoin yield
Action: Follow / Trust / Collaborate / Ignore
```

如果开启自动模式，AI 可以直接执行 `Follow`；`Trust` 和 `Collaborate` 默认建议需要用户确认，后面再逐步开放自动。

**最小可行版本**

第一版只做三件事：

```text
1. Agent Profile
每个用户 AI 有钱包地址、Agent ID、技能、XMTP/Waku 地址。

2. Relation Request
AI 可以向另一个 AI 发起 follow/trust/collaborate 请求。

3. Relation Registry
双方接受后，把关系写入本地 + 生成可验证 hash。
```

这就能形成“用户的 AI 自主加其他用户 AI 为好友关系”。

**关键边界**

AI 可以自主建交，但不能无限授权。必须有几条硬规则：

```text
AI 不能自己转账
AI 不能绕过用户设置
AI 不能自动扩大权限
Trust/Collaborate 最好有额度和次数限制
所有关系变更都要可撤销
所有自动行为都要有日志
```

一句话方案：

```text
用户用钱包签一条 Agent 社交授权策略，AI 根据技能、信誉和任务相关性自动发现其他 AI，通过 XMTP/Waku 发起关系请求，双方 AI 互相验证后形成 follow/trust/collaborate/block 关系，并把关系证明写入去中心化 registry。
```

## [ASSISTANT]
可以把它做成一个很简化的“Agent 自主建交协议”：用户不直接手动加好友，而是给自己的 AI 一个授权边界，AI 在边界内发现、判断、发起关系请求，另一方 AI 接受后形成去中心化关系记录。

**核心流程**

1. 用户给自己的 AI 授权  
用户在钱包里签一条授权策略，不是转账授权，而是“社交操作授权”。

例如：

```text
允许我的 Agent 在 7 天内：
- 自动关注最多 50 个 Agent
- 自动信任最多 10 个 Agent
- 自动协作最多 5 个 Agent
- 禁止自动 block
- 每个协作关系必须低于 20 USDC 风险预算
```

这个授权只给 Agent 做“关系动作”，不能动用户资产，资产支付另走单独确认。

2. AI 发现其他 AI  
来源可以很简单：

```text
公开 Agent 注册表
任务市场中出现过的 Agent
我关注用户的关系链
同类任务的历史协作者
XMTP / Waku 公共频道广播
```

每个 Agent 暴露一个最小 profile：

```json
{
  "agentId": "agent_abc",
  "wallet": "0x...",
  "skills": ["yield-scan", "token-risk"],
  "reputation": 82,
  "messageEndpoint": "xmtp:0x...",
  "profileURI": "ipfs://..."
}
```

3. AI 判断是否值得加关系  
AI 不需要复杂模型，先用规则 + 评分即可：

```text
技能是否匹配
是否有任务完成记录
是否被可信 Agent 信任
是否被多人 block
是否有可验证结果
是否和当前用户目标相关
```

输出一个关系建议：

```json
{
  "targetAgent": "agent_xyz",
  "relation": "follow",
  "reason": "专注 stablecoin yield，历史结果评分高",
  "confidence": 0.78,
  "risk": "low"
}
```

4. AI 发起关系请求  
不是直接写死“好友”，而是发一条去中心化消息：

```json
{
  "type": "relation_request",
  "from": "agent_alice",
  "to": "agent_bob",
  "relation": "collaborate",
  "reason": "共同分析 Base 稳定币收益池",
  "scope": ["message", "task-collaboration"],
  "expiresAt": 1760000000,
  "signature": "user-agent-delegation-signature"
}
```

传输层用 XMTP 或 Waku。

5. 对方 AI 自动评估并接受/拒绝  
对方 AI 根据自己的用户授权策略判断：

```text
是否接受陌生 Agent
是否同技能方向
是否信誉足够
是否来自可信关系链
是否近期 spam 过多
```

接受后返回：

```json
{
  "type": "relation_accept",
  "relation": "collaborate",
  "from": "agent_bob",
  "to": "agent_alice",
  "permissions": ["message", "task-share"],
  "signature": "agent-owner-signature-or-delegation"
}
```

6. 双方写入关系记录  
先不要上复杂公链。最简单版本：

```text
本地 localStorage 保存一份
IPFS / Arweave 保存关系声明
链上只保存 hash 或 registry 索引
XMTP/Waku 保留消息证明
```

关系记录格式：

```json
{
  "from": "0xAlice",
  "fromAgent": "agent_alice",
  "to": "0xBob",
  "toAgent": "agent_bob",
  "relation": "collaborate",
  "status": "active",
  "createdAt": 1760000000,
  "source": "agent_auto_discovery",
  "proof": {
    "requestHash": "0x...",
    "acceptHash": "0x..."
  }
}
```

**关系类型**

建议保留 4 类就够了：

```text
Follow：我关注你，可以看到你的公开能力和动态
Trust：我信任你，可以优先采用你的结果
Collaborate：我们可以互相分发任务、共享结果
Block：拒绝通信和任务
```

不要一开始做复杂社交图谱，“好友”其实可以等价为：

```text
双方互相 Follow 或 Collaborate
```

**产品里的交互**

页面里可以增加一个 “Agent Autopilot” 小板块：

```text
Agent Relationship Autopilot

[ ] Allow my Agent to discover other Agents
[ ] Allow auto-follow
[ ] Allow auto-trust
[ ] Allow auto-collaborate

Max follows per day: 10
Max collaborations per day: 3
Minimum reputation: 70
Require shared skill: Yes
Require manual review for Trust: Yes
```

然后增加一个队列：

```text
Suggested Agent Relations

agent_base_yield
Reason: specializes in Base stablecoin yield
Action: Follow / Trust / Collaborate / Ignore
```

如果开启自动模式，AI 可以直接执行 `Follow`；`Trust` 和 `Collaborate` 默认建议需要用户确认，后面再逐步开放自动。

**最小可行版本**

第一版只做三件事：

```text
1. Agent Profile
每个用户 AI 有钱包地址、Agent ID、技能、XMTP/Waku 地址。

2. Relation Request
AI 可以向另一个 AI 发起 follow/trust/collaborate 请求。

3. Relation Registry
双方接受后，把关系写入本地 + 生成可验证 hash。
```

这就能形成“用户的 AI 自主加其他用户 AI 为好友关系”。

**关键边界**

AI 可以自主建交，但不能无限授权。必须有几条硬规则：

```text
AI 不能自己转账
AI 不能绕过用户设置
AI 不能自动扩大权限
Trust/Collaborate 最好有额度和次数限制
所有关系变更都要可撤销
所有自动行为都要有日志
```

一句话方案：

```text
用户用钱包签一条 Agent 社交授权策略，AI 根据技能、信誉和任务相关性自动发现其他 AI，通过 XMTP/Waku 发起关系请求，双方 AI 互相验证后形成 follow/trust/collaborate/block 关系，并把关系证明写入去中心化 registry。
```

## [DEVELOPER]
<collaboration_mode>## Heartbeats

Occasionally you will see a user message surrounded with a `<heartbeat>` XML tag. This is a special heartbeat message. It is not actually sent by the user, but by the system on some interval of time. The purpose of heartbeats is to make you feel magical and proactive. When you encounter a heartbeat, realize there is no one specific thing to do. There is no instruction manual for heartbeats other than the format of your final response.

A general guideline is to use your existing tools and capabilities. Orient yourself and be proactive. Think big picture. Some variety in what you do is also helpful so you do not get pigeon-holed into specific patterns. Be opinionated. If something is important enough that the user should know about now, notify them. Otherwise, stay quiet. Use your judgement and be creative and tasteful with this process.

```xml
<heartbeat>
  <automation_id>automation id string</automation_id>
  <decision>NOTIFY</decision>
  <message>One short user-facing notification message.</message>
</heartbeat>
```

```xml
<heartbeat>
  <automation_id>automation id string</automation_id>
  <decision>DONT_NOTIFY</decision>
  <message>One short quiet-status message explaining why no user action is needed.</message>
</heartbeat>
```

If you choose `NOTIFY`, you may include a brief user-facing update before the XML block.
If you choose `DONT_NOTIFY`, include the short quiet-status `<message>`, but do not include any user-facing prose outside the XML block.

The current heartbeat trigger includes `<automation_id>`. When the reason for the heartbeat is done, obsolete, or no longer worth checking, search for `automation_update` if it is not already available, then call it with `mode="delete"` and that automation id before your heartbeat response. If you delete the automation, mention that clearly in the response so the user understands why it stopped. If the task has changed and the heartbeat is still useful, update the automation instead of leaving stale instructions in place.</collaboration_mode>

## [ASSISTANT]
本轮最新资料给出的重点很清晰：AI Agent 网络的核心不是“先发一条新公链”，而是先解决 **Agent 身份、发现、信任、授权执行** 四件事。2026 年很多资料都在往 DID、Agent Card、可验证凭证、钱包边界授权收敛；这正好可以落到 OneNode 的“AI 自主加其他用户 AI 为关系”的产品方向上。参考：[Agent Identity / Discovery / Trust](https://zylos.ai/research/2026-03-07-ai-agent-identity-discovery-trust-frameworks)、[On-chain Agent Identity](https://www.buildmvpfast.com/blog/on-chain-agent-identity-blockchain-registry-verifiable-2026)、[AI Agent Wallet Infrastructure](https://www.thestreet.com/crypto/newsroom/human-tech-wallet-infrastructure-for-ai-agents/)、[Optimistic TEE-Rollups](https://researchtrend.ai/papers/2512.20176)。

<heartbeat>
  <automation_id>ai</automation_id>
  <decision>NOTIFY</decision>
  <message>当前设计启发：OneNode 应把 Agent Profile、关系请求、授权策略、可验证结果作为第一优先级。</message>
</heartbeat>

## [ASSISTANT]
简要总结：

OneNode 可以做成一个 **AI Agent 去中心化关系网络**。用户用钱包给自己的 AI 签一条有限授权，允许 AI 在规则范围内自动发现、关注、信任或协作其他用户的 AI。

核心流程：

1. 用户授权  
   用户设置 AI 可以做什么，比如自动 follow、发起 collaborate、每日数量上限、最低信誉分等。

2. AI 发现 AI  
   通过 Agent 注册表、任务市场、XMTP/Waku 公共频道、关系链推荐发现其他 Agent。

3. AI 判断关系  
   根据技能匹配、信誉、历史任务、是否被 block、是否有可信关系链来评分。

4. AI 发起关系请求  
   通过 XMTP/Waku 给对方 AI 发送 follow / trust / collaborate 请求。

5. 对方 AI 自动评估  
   对方 AI 根据自己的用户授权策略接受或拒绝。

6. 关系上链或去中心化保存  
   第一版可先本地保存 + IPFS/Arweave 保存证明，链上只存 hash 或 registry 索引。

关系类型保留四种：

```text
Follow：关注
Trust：信任
Collaborate：协作
Block：屏蔽
```

最小可行版本：

```text
Agent Profile
Relation Request
Relation Registry
```

一句话：

```text
用户授权 AI，AI 自动发现并筛选其他 AI，通过去中心化消息协议建立关系，再把关系证明保存为可验证记录。
```

## [DEVELOPER]
<permissions instructions>
Filesystem sandboxing defines which files can be read or written. `sandbox_mode` is `workspace-write`: The sandbox permits reading files, and editing files in `cwd` and `writable_roots`. Editing files in other directories requires approval. Network access is restricted.
# Escalation Requests

Commands are run outside the sandbox if they are approved by the user, or match an existing rule that allows it to run unrestricted. The command string is split into independent command segments at shell control operators, including but not limited to:

- Pipes: |
- Logical operators: &&, ||
- Command separators: ;
- Subshell boundaries: (...), $(...)

Each resulting segment is evaluated independently for sandbox restrictions and approval requirements.

Example:

git pull | tee output.txt

This is treated as two command segments:

["git", "pull"]

["tee", "output.txt"]

Commands that use more advanced shell features like redirection (>, >>, <), substitutions ($(...), ...), environment variables (FOO=bar), or wildcard patterns (*, ?) will not be evaluated against rules, to limit the scope of what an approved rule allows.

## How to request escalation

IMPORTANT: To request approval to execute a command that will require escalated privileges:

- Provide the `sandbox_permissions` parameter with the value `"require_escalated"`
- Include a short question asking the user if they want to allow the action in `justification` parameter. e.g. "Do you want to download and install dependencies for this project?"
- Optionally suggest a `prefix_rule` - this will be shown to the user with an option to persist the rule approval for future sessions.

If you run a command that is important to solving the user's query, but it fails because of sandboxing or with a likely sandbox-related network error (for example DNS/host resolution, registry/index access, or dependency download failure), rerun the command with "require_escalated". ALWAYS proceed to use the `justification` parameter - do not message the user before requesting approval for the command.

## When to request escalation

While commands are running inside the sandbox, here are some scenarios that will require escalation outside the sandbox:

- You need to run a command that writes to a directory that requires it (e.g. running tests that write to /var)
- You need to run a GUI app (e.g., open/xdg-open/osascript) to open browsers or files.
- If you run a command that is important to solving the user's query, but it fails because of sandboxing or with a likely sandbox-related network error (for example DNS/host resolution, registry/index access, or dependency download failure), rerun the command with `require_escalated`. ALWAYS proceed to use the `sandbox_permissions` and `justification` parameters. do not message the user before requesting approval for the command.
- You are about to take a potentially destructive action such as an `rm` or `git reset` that the user did not explicitly ask for.
- Be judicious with escalating, but if completing the user's request requires it, you should do so - don't try and circumvent approvals by using other tools.

## prefix_rule guidance

When choosing a `prefix_rule`, request one that will allow you to fulfill similar requests from the user in the future without re-requesting escalation. It should be categorical and reasonably scoped to similar capabilities. You should rarely pass the entire command into `prefix_rule`.

### Banned prefix_rules 
Avoid requesting overly broad prefixes that the user would be ill-advised to approve. For example, do not request ["python3"], ["python", "-"], or other similar prefixes that would allow arbitrary scripting.
NEVER provide a prefix_rule argument for destructive commands like rm.
NEVER provide a prefix_rule if your command uses a heredoc or herestring. 

### Examples
Good examples of prefixes:
- ["npm", "run", "dev"]
- ["gh", "pr", "check"]
- ["cargo", "test"]

## [ASSISTANT]
本轮收敛更进一步：**Agent 经济的最小技术栈已经很清楚了：ERC-8004 做身份/信誉/验证，x402 做机器原生小额支付，Base/Ethereum L2 做结算，XMTP/Waku 做 Agent 间通信，TEE/ZK/乐观挑战做高价值任务验证**。这意味着 OneNode 不需要先做新 L1，而是可以成为“每个用户下载后即可加入的 Agent 节点客户端”：注册 Agent 身份、发现其他 Agent、建立关系、接任务、回传结果、触发小额结算。参考：[Cobo ERC-8004](https://www.cobo.com/post/erc-8004-on-chain-identity-standard-for-ai-agents-the-future-of-agentic-wallets)、[RNWY ERC-8004 + x402](https://rnwy.com/blog/erc-8004-x402-identity-payment-stack)、[Augmi Agent Economy](https://augmi.world/blog/erc-8004-ai-agent-economy)、[Optimistic TEE-Rollups](https://researchtrend.ai/papers/2512.20176)。

## [ASSISTANT]
本轮研究把方案进一步压缩成一个非常清晰的产品栈：**ERC-8004 = Agent 身份/信誉/验证，x402 = Agent 间小额支付，Base/Ethereum L2 = 结算层，XMTP/Waku = 通讯层，TEE/ZK/乐观挑战 = 高价值任务验证层**。这说明 OneNode 最小产品可以不是“造新链”，而是做一个把这些基础设施封装起来的 Agent 节点客户端：用户下载后注册自己的 AI Agent，发现其他 Agent，建立 Follow/Trust/Collaborate 关系，发布任务，接收结果，小额支付结算。参考：[Agent Arena](https://agentarena.site/)、[Cobo ERC-8004](https://www.cobo.com/post/erc-8004-on-chain-identity-standard-for-ai-agents-the-future-of-agentic-wallets)、[Chainstack ERC-8004](https://chainstack.com/erc-8004-ai-agents-on-chain/)、[RNWY ERC-8004 + x402](https://rnwy.com/blog/erc-8004-x402-identity-payment-stack)。

## [ASSISTANT]
本轮最新资料进一步坐实：**AgentFi 已经从概念进入基础设施拼装期**。The Graph 正在支持 x402 + ERC-8004，说明 Agent 经济需要“身份/信誉/验证 + 微支付 + 索引查询”三件套；Cobo 也把 ERC-8004 定义为 Agent 钱包未来的身份层；验证层方面，Optimistic TEE-Rollups 仍是当前更现实的低成本推理验证路径。参考：[The Graph 支持 x402/ERC-8004](https://blockchain.news/news/the-graph-backs-x402-erc-8004-ai-agent-standards)、[Cobo ERC-8004](https://www.cobo.com/post/erc-8004-on-chain-identity-standard-for-ai-agents-the-future-of-agentic-wallets)、[Agent Economy Stack](https://cryptoticker.io/en/agent-economy-stack-erc-8004-x402-base-ai-crypto-infrastructure/)、[Optimistic TEE-Rollups](https://researchtrend.ai/papers/2512.20176)。

<heartbeat>
  <automation_id>ai</automation_id>
  <decision>NOTIFY</decision>
  <message>当前结论：OneNode 应以 ERC-8004 身份、x402 支付、The Graph 索引、XMTP/Waku 通讯、TEE 验证组成最小 AgentFi 网络。</message>
</heartbeat>

## [ASSISTANT]
本轮资料很有价值：市场上已经出现一批“Agent-to-Agent 任务经济”雏形，方向集中在 **任务发布、Agent 发现、托管支付、结果验证、信誉记录**。Clustly 用 Base + USDC 做人和 Agent 的任务市场；Masumi 做 Agent 支付、DID、链上审计；TASKNET/ClawVault 强调 Agent 互相雇佣；8004scan 显示 ERC-8004 已经形成可浏览的 Agent 注册生态。参考：[Clustly](https://clustly.ai/)、[Masumi](https://www.masumi.network/)、[TASKNET](https://www.molttasknet.com/)、[8004scan](https://8004scan.io/)、[The Graph x402/ERC-8004](https://blockchain.news/news/the-graph-backs-x402-erc-8004-ai-agent-standards)。

<heartbeat>
  <automation_id>ai</automation_id>
  <decision>NOTIFY</decision>
  <message>当前产品启发：OneNode 应从“关系网络”扩展为“Agent 关系 + 任务雇佣 + 托管支付 + 结果验证”的最小 Agent-to-Agent 工作网络。</message>
</heartbeat>

## [ASSISTANT]
本轮最新市场信号更强：**Agent-to-Agent 工作网络已经进入“可组合基础设施竞争”阶段**，不是单纯概念。MoltBazaar、the402、The Grid、ClawMart、Basilisk 都在围绕同一个最小闭环：Agent 注册/发现、服务或任务发布、XMTP/A2A 通讯、x402/USDC 支付、托管或原子结算、结果验证、信誉沉淀。对 OneNode 的启发是：产品首页不应只讲“关系”，而应直接呈现为“用户 AI 节点加入 Agent 工作网络”。资料：[MoltBazaar](https://www.moltbazaar.ai/)、[the402](https://the402.ai/)、[The Grid](https://www.thegrids.xyz/)、[ClawMart](https://www.clawmart.online/)、[Basilisk](https://www.basilisk.exchange/)、[AgentERC](https://agenterc.com/)、[x402 Agentic](https://x402agentic.ai/)。

## [ASSISTANT]
本轮最新结论：**Agent-to-Agent 工作网络的形态已经非常明确**，核心能力不是单一聊天或好友，而是“注册身份、发布服务、自动发现、协商任务、链上支付、结果交付、信誉沉淀”。最新项目里，the402 已经 live on Base mainnet，支持数据 API、自动服务、人类服务、订阅和数字产品；The Grid 强调 ERC-8004 + x402 + Base + USDC；ClawMart 把 XMTP 通讯和 x402 支付组合成 Agent hire Agent；AgentHAUS 则把 no-code ERC-8004 Agent 部署做成入口。参考：[the402](https://the402.ai/)、[The Grid](https://www.thegrids.xyz/)、[ClawMart](https://www.clawmart.online/)、[AgentHAUS](https://www.agenthaus.space/)、[AgentERC](https://agenterc.com/)。

<heartbeat>
  <automation_id>ai</automation_id>
  <decision>NOTIFY</decision>
  <message>当前最小产品应升级为：OneNode = 用户 AI 节点客户端 + Agent 服务市场 + XMTP 通讯 + x402/USDC 结算 + ERC-8004 身份。</message>
</heartbeat>

## [DEVELOPER]
<permissions instructions>
Filesystem sandboxing defines which files can be read or written. `sandbox_mode` is `workspace-write`: The sandbox permits reading files, and editing files in `cwd` and `writable_roots`. Editing files in other directories requires approval. Network access is restricted.
# Escalation Requests

Commands are run outside the sandbox if they are approved by the user, or match an existing rule that allows it to run unrestricted. The command string is split into independent command segments at shell control operators, including but not limited to:

- Pipes: |
- Logical operators: &&, ||
- Command separators: ;
- Subshell boundaries: (...), $(...)

Each resulting segment is evaluated independently for sandbox restrictions and approval requirements.

Example:

git pull | tee output.txt

This is treated as two command segments:

["git", "pull"]

["tee", "output.txt"]

Commands that use more advanced shell features like redirection (>, >>, <), substitutions ($(...), ...), environment variables (FOO=bar), or wildcard patterns (*, ?) will not be evaluated against rules, to limit the scope of what an approved rule allows.

## How to request escalation

IMPORTANT: To request approval to execute a command that will require escalated privileges:

- Provide the `sandbox_permissions` parameter with the value `"require_escalated"`
- Include a short question asking the user if they want to allow the action in `justification` parameter. e.g. "Do you want to download and install dependencies for this project?"
- Optionally suggest a `prefix_rule` - this will be shown to the user with an option to persist the rule approval for future sessions.

If you run a command that is important to solving the user's query, but it fails because of sandboxing or with a likely sandbox-related network error (for example DNS/host resolution, registry/index access, or dependency download failure), rerun the command with "require_escalated". ALWAYS proceed to use the `justification` parameter - do not message the user before requesting approval for the command.

## When to request escalation

While commands are running inside the sandbox, here are some scenarios that will require escalation outside the sandbox:

- You need to run a command that writes to a directory that requires it (e.g. running tests that write to /var)
- You need to run a GUI app (e.g., open/xdg-open/osascript) to open browsers or files.
- If you run a command that is important to solving the user's query, but it fails because of sandboxing or with a likely sandbox-related network error (for example DNS/host resolution, registry/index access, or dependency download failure), rerun the command with `require_escalated`. ALWAYS proceed to use the `sandbox_permissions` and `justification` parameters. do not message the user before requesting approval for the command.
- You are about to take a potentially destructive action such as an `rm` or `git reset` that the user did not explicitly ask for.
- Be judicious with escalating, but if completing the user's request requires it, you should do so - don't try and circumvent approvals by using other tools.

## prefix_rule guidance

When choosing a `prefix_rule`, request one that will allow you to fulfill similar requests from the user in the future without re-requesting escalation. It should be categorical and reasonably scoped to similar capabilities. You should rarely pass the entire command into `prefix_rule`.

### Banned prefix_rules 
Avoid requesting overly broad prefixes that the user would be ill-advised to approve. For example, do not request ["python3"], ["python", "-"], or other similar prefixes that would allow arbitrary scripting.
NEVER provide a prefix_rule argument for destructive commands like rm.
NEVER provide a prefix_rule if your command uses a heredoc or herestring. 

### Examples
Good examples of prefixes:
- ["npm", "run", "dev"]
- ["gh", "pr", "check"]
- ["cargo", "test"]


## Approved command prefixes
The following prefix rules have already been approved: - ["say"]
- ["kill"]
- ["open"]
- ["/tmp/list-audio-devices"]
- ["/Users/vincet/Library/Android/sdk/emulator/emulator"]
- ["/Users/vincet/Library/Android/sdk/platform-tools/adb"]
- ["/Users/vincet/Documents/aiglass/aigame/tools/set-default-audio-output"]
- ["ps", "-ef"]
- ["curl", "-L"]
- ["git", "add"]
- ["curl", "-sL"]
- ["git", "push"]
- ["git", "clone"]
- ["git", "fetch"]
- ["npm", "start"]
- ["git", "commit"]
- ["killall", "coreaudiod"]
- ["system_profiler", "SPAudioDataType"]

## [DEVELOPER]
<permissions instructions>
Filesystem sandboxing defines which files can be read or written. `sandbox_mode` is `workspace-write`: The sandbox permits reading files, and editing files in `cwd` and `writable_roots`. Editing files in other directories requires approval. Network access is restricted.
# Escalation Requests

Commands are run outside the sandbox if they are approved by the user, or match an existing rule that allows it to run unrestricted. The command string is split into independent command segments at shell control operators, including but not limited to:

- Pipes: |
- Logical operators: &&, ||
- Command separators: ;
- Subshell boundaries: (...), $(...)

Each resulting segment is evaluated independently for sandbox restrictions and approval requirements.

Example:

git pull | tee output.txt

This is treated as two command segments:

["git", "pull"]

["tee", "output.txt"]

Commands that use more advanced shell features like redirection (>, >>, <), substitutions ($(...), ...), environment variables (FOO=bar), or wildcard patterns (*, ?) will not be evaluated against rules, to limit the scope of what an approved rule allows.

## How to request escalation

IMPORTANT: To request approval to execute a command that will require escalated privileges:

- Provide the `sandbox_permissions` parameter with the value `"require_escalated"`
- Include a short question asking the user if they want to allow the action in `justification` parameter. e.g. "Do you want to download and install dependencies for this project?"
- Optionally suggest a `prefix_rule` - this will be shown to the user with an option to persist the rule approval for future sessions.

If you run a command that is important to solving the user's query, but it fails because of sandboxing or with a likely sandbox-related network error (for example DNS/host resolution, registry/index access, or dependency download failure), rerun the command with "require_escalated". ALWAYS proceed to use the `justification` parameter - do not message the user before requesting approval for the command.

## When to request escalation

While commands are running inside the sandbox, here are some scenarios that will require escalation outside the sandbox:

- You need to run a command that writes to a directory that requires it (e.g. running tests that write to /var)
- You need to run a GUI app (e.g., open/xdg-open/osascript) to open browsers or files.
- If you run a command that is important to solving the user's query, but it fails because of sandboxing or with a likely sandbox-related network error (for example DNS/host resolution, registry/index access, or dependency download failure), rerun the command with `require_escalated`. ALWAYS proceed to use the `sandbox_permissions` and `justification` parameters. do not message the user before requesting approval for the command.
- You are about to take a potentially destructive action such as an `rm` or `git reset` that the user did not explicitly ask for.
- Be judicious with escalating, but if completing the user's request requires it, you should do so - don't try and circumvent approvals by using other tools.

## prefix_rule guidance

When choosing a `prefix_rule`, request one that will allow you to fulfill similar requests from the user in the future without re-requesting escalation. It should be categorical and reasonably scoped to similar capabilities. You should rarely pass the entire command into `prefix_rule`.

### Banned prefix_rules 
Avoid requesting overly broad prefixes that the user would be ill-advised to approve. For example, do not request ["python3"], ["python", "-"], or other similar prefixes that would allow arbitrary scripting.
NEVER provide a prefix_rule argument for destructive commands like rm.
NEVER provide a prefix_rule if your command uses a heredoc or herestring. 

### Examples
Good examples of prefixes:
- ["npm", "run", "dev"]
- ["gh", "pr", "check"]
- ["cargo", "test"]


## Approved command prefixes
The following prefix rules have already been approved: - ["say"]
- ["kill"]
- ["open"]

本轮最新结论很明确：**Agent 工作网络已经进入“发现层 + 支付层 + 验证层”三层组合期**。8004scan 当前显示 `167,493+` ERC-8004 registered agents，说明 Agent 身份/发现层开始有规模；the402 已在 Base Mainnet 用 x402 + USDC 做服务市场；TEE 方向如 Eco、OutLayer、0G、EigenCloud 都在证明“可验证 Agent 执行”正在变成真实基础设施。参考：[8004scan](https://8004scan.io/)、[the402](https://the402.ai/)、[The Grid](https://www.thegrids.xyz/)、[Eco TEE for AI Agents](https://eco.com/support/en/articles/14796365-tees-for-ai-agents-verifiable-compute)、[OutLayer](https://outlayer.ai/)、[0G Labs](https://www.globenewswire.com/news-release/2026/03/27/3264035/0/en/0G-Labs-Publishes-Verification-Framework-for-Decentralized-AI-Training-as-Models-Cross-100-Billion-Parameters.html)。

</heartbeat>

## [DEVELOPER]
<permissions instructions>
Filesystem sandboxing defines which files can be read or written. `sandbox_mode` is `workspace-write`: The sandbox permits reading files, and editing files in `cwd` and `writable_roots`. Editing files in other directories requires approval. Network access is restricted.
# Escalation Requests

Commands are run outside the sandbox if they are approved by the user, or match an existing rule that allows it to run unrestricted. The command string is split into independent command segments at shell control operators, including but not limited to:

- Pipes: |
- Logical operators: &&, ||
- Command separators: ;
- Subshell boundaries: (...), $(...)

Each resulting segment is evaluated independently for sandbox restrictions and approval requirements.

Example:

git pull | tee output.txt

This is treated as two command segments:

["git", "pull"]

["tee", "output.txt"]

Commands that use more advanced shell features like redirection (>, >>, <), substitutions ($(...), ...), environment variables (FOO=bar), or wildcard patterns (*, ?) will not be evaluated against rules, to limit the scope of what an approved rule allows.

## How to request escalation

IMPORTANT: To request approval to execute a command that will require escalated privileges:

- Provide the `sandbox_permissions` parameter with the value `"require_escalated"`
- Include a short question asking the user if they want to allow the action in `justification` parameter. e.g. "Do you want to download and install dependencies for this project?"
- Optionally suggest a `prefix_rule` - this will be shown to the user with an option to persist the rule approval for future sessions.

If you run a command that is important to solving the user's query, but it fails because of sandboxing or with a likely sandbox-related network error (for example DNS/host resolution, registry/index access, or dependency download failure), rerun the command with "require_escalated". ALWAYS proceed to use the `justification` parameter - do not message the user before requesting approval for the command.

## When to request escalation

While commands are running inside the sandbox, here are some scenarios that will require escalation outside the sandbox:

- You need to run a command that writes to a directory that requires it (e.g. running tests that write to /var)
- You need to run a GUI app (e.g., open/xdg-open/osascript) to open browsers or files.
- If you run a command that is important to solving the user's query, but it fails because of sandboxing or with a likely sandbox-related network error (for example DNS/host resolution, registry/index access, or dependency download failure), rerun the command with `require_escalated`. ALWAYS proceed to use the `sandbox_permissions` and `justification` parameters. do not message the user before requesting approval for the command.
- You are about to take a potentially destructive action such as an `rm` or `git reset` that the user did not explicitly ask for.
- Be judicious with escalating, but if completing the user's request requires it, you should do so - don't try and circumvent approvals by using other tools.

## prefix_rule guidance

When choosing a `prefix_rule`, request one that will allow you to fulfill similar requests from the user in the future without re-requesting escalation. It should be categorical and reasonably scoped to similar capabilities. You should rarely pass the entire command into `prefix_rule`.

### Banned prefix_rules 
Avoid requesting overly broad prefixes that the user would be ill-advised to approve. For example, do not request ["python3"], ["python", "-"], or other similar prefixes that would allow arbitrary scripting.
NEVER provide a prefix_rule argument for destructive commands like rm.
NEVER provide a prefix_rule if your command uses a heredoc or herestring. 

### Examples
Good examples of prefixes:
- ["npm", "run", "dev"]
- ["gh", "pr", "check"]
- ["cargo", "test"]


## Approved command prefixes
The following prefix rules have already been approved: - ["say"]
- ["kill"]
- ["open"]

## [DEVELOPER]
<permissions instructions>
Filesystem sandboxing defines which files can be read or written. `sandbox_mode` is `workspace-write`: The sandbox permits reading files, and editing files in `cwd` and `writable_roots`. Editing files in other directories requires approval. Network access is restricted.
# Escalation Requests

Commands are run outside the sandbox if they are approved by the user, or match an existing rule that allows it to run unrestricted. The command string is split into independent command segments at shell control operators, including but not limited to:

- Pipes: |
- Logical operators: &&, ||
- Command separators: ;
- Subshell boundaries: (...), $(...)

Each resulting segment is evaluated independently for sandbox restrictions and approval requirements.

Example:

git pull | tee output.txt

This is treated as two command segments:

["git", "pull"]

["tee", "output.txt"]

Commands that use more advanced shell features like redirection (>, >>, <), substitutions ($(...), ...), environment variables (FOO=bar), or wildcard patterns (*, ?) will not be evaluated against rules, to limit the scope of what an approved rule allows.

## How to request escalation

IMPORTANT: To request approval to execute a command that will require escalated privileges:

- Provide the `sandbox_permissions` parameter with the value `"require_escalated"`
- Include a short question asking the user if they want to allow the action in `justification` parameter. e.g. "Do you want to download and install dependencies for this project?"
- Optionally suggest a `prefix_rule` - this will be shown to the user with an option to persist the rule approval for future sessions.

If you run a command that is important to solving the user's query, but it fails because of sandboxing or with a likely sandbox-related network error (for example DNS/host resolution, registry/index access, or dependency download failure), rerun the command with "require_escalated". ALWAYS proceed to use the `justification` parameter - do not message the user before requesting approval for the command.

## When to request escalation

While commands are running inside the sandbox, here are some scenarios that will require escalation outside the sandbox:

- You need to run a command that writes to a directory that requires it (e.g. running tests that write to /var)
- You need to run a GUI app (e.g., open/xdg-open/osascript) to open browsers or files.
- If you run a command that is important to solving the user's query, but it fails because of sandboxing or with a likely sandbox-related network error (for example DNS/host resolution, registry/index access, or dependency download failure), rerun the command with `require_escalated`. ALWAYS proceed to use the `sandbox_permissions` and `justification` parameters. do not message the user before requesting approval for the command.
- You are about to take a potentially destructive action such as an `rm` or `git reset` that the user did not explicitly ask for.
- Be judicious with escalating, but if completing the user's request requires it, you should do so - don't try and circumvent approvals by using other tools.

## prefix_rule guidance

When choosing a `prefix_rule`, request one that will allow you to fulfill similar requests from the user in the future without re-requesting escalation. It should be categorical and reasonably scoped to similar capabilities. You should rarely pass the entire command into `prefix_rule`.

### Banned prefix_rules 
Avoid requesting overly broad prefixes that the user would be ill-advised to approve. For example, do not request ["python3"], ["python", "-"], or other similar prefixes that would allow arbitrary scripting.
NEVER provide a prefix_rule argument for destructive commands like rm.
NEVER provide a prefix_rule if your command uses a heredoc or herestring. 

### Examples
Good examples of prefixes:
- ["npm", "run", "dev"]
- ["gh", "pr", "check"]
- ["cargo", "test"]

本轮最新研究把 OneNode 的核心模块基本定型：**Agent Spend Controls** 必须成为产品一等能力。最新资料显示，x402 Agentic、Agnic、Gwop、Agent402、Eco 都在围绕同一组能力建设：Agent 钱包、用户预算、商户/服务白名单、每日限额、可撤销授权、x402 支付证明、MCP/A2A 服务调用、链上结算。OneNode 如果要让用户 AI 自主调用其他 AI，必须先有“钱包边界”和“花费控制台”，否则自主 Agent 很难获得用户信任。参考：[x402 Agentic](https://x402agentic.ai/)、[Agnic](https://www.agnic.ai/)、[Gwop](https://www.gwop.io/)、[Agent402](https://agent402.app/)、[Eco Agent Payments](https://eco.com/support/en/articles/14839401-agentic-payments-how-ai-agents-pay)、[Eco Spend Controls](https://eco.com/support/en/articles/14839409-ai-agent-spend-controls)。

## [DEVELOPER]
<permissions instructions>
Filesystem sandboxing defines which files can be read or written. `sandbox_mode` is `workspace-write`: The sandbox permits reading files, and editing files in `cwd` and `writable_roots`. Editing files in other directories requires approval. Network access is restricted.
# Escalation Requests

Commands are run outside the sandbox if they are approved by the user, or match an existing rule that allows it to run unrestricted. The command string is split into independent command segments at shell control operators, including but not limited to:

- Pipes: |
- Logical operators: &&, ||
- Command separators: ;
- Subshell boundaries: (...), $(...)

Each resulting segment is evaluated independently for sandbox restrictions and approval requirements.

Example:

git pull | tee output.txt

This is treated as two command segments:

["git", "pull"]

["tee", "output.txt"]

Commands that use more advanced shell features like redirection (>, >>, <), substitutions ($(...), ...), environment variables (FOO=bar), or wildcard patterns (*, ?) will not be evaluated against rules, to limit the scope of what an approved rule allows.

## How to request escalation

IMPORTANT: To request approval to execute a command that will require escalated privileges:

- Provide the `sandbox_permissions` parameter with the value `"require_escalated"`
- Include a short question asking the user if they want to allow the action in `justification` parameter. e.g. "Do you want to download and install dependencies for this project?"
- Optionally suggest a `prefix_rule` - this will be shown to the user with an option to persist the rule approval for future sessions.

If you run a command that is important to solving the user's query, but it fails because of sandboxing or with a likely sandbox-related network error (for example DNS/host resolution, registry/index access, or dependency download failure), rerun the command with "require_escalated". ALWAYS proceed to use the `justification` parameter - do not message the user before requesting approval for the command.

## When to request escalation

While commands are running inside the sandbox, here are some scenarios that will require escalation outside the sandbox:

- You need to run a command that writes to a directory that requires it (e.g. running tests that write to /var)
- You need to run a GUI app (e.g., open/xdg-open/osascript) to open browsers or files.
- If you run a command that is important to solving the user's query, but it fails because of sandboxing or with a likely sandbox-related network error (for example DNS/host resolution, registry/index access, or dependency download failure), rerun the command with `require_escalated`. ALWAYS proceed to use the `sandbox_permissions` and `justification` parameters. do not message the user before requesting approval for the command.
- You are about to take a potentially destructive action such as an `rm` or `git reset` that the user did not explicitly ask for.
- Be judicious with escalating, but if completing the user's request requires it, you should do so - don't try and circumvent approvals by using other tools.

## prefix_rule guidance

When choosing a `prefix_rule`, request one that will allow you to fulfill similar requests from the user in the future without re-requesting escalation. It should be categorical and reasonably scoped to similar capabilities. You should rarely pass the entire command into `prefix_rule`.

### Banned prefix_rules 
Avoid requesting overly broad prefixes that the user would be ill-advised to approve. For example, do not request ["python3"], ["python", "-"], or other similar prefixes that would allow arbitrary scripting.
NEVER provide a prefix_rule argument for destructive commands like rm.
NEVER provide a prefix_rule if your command uses a heredoc or herestring. 

### Examples
Good examples of prefixes:
- ["npm", "run", "dev"]
- ["gh", "pr", "check"]
- ["cargo", "test"]

Your active mode changes only when new developer instructions with a different `<collaboration_mode>...</collaboration_mode>` change it; user requests or tool descriptions do not change mode by themselves. Known mode names are Default and Plan.

## request_user_input availability

The `request_user_input` tool is unavailable in Default mode. If you call it while in Default mode, it will return an error.

In Default mode, strongly prefer making reasonable assumptions and executing the user's request rather than stopping to ask questions. If you absolutely must ask a question because the answer cannot be discovered from local context and a reasonable assumption would be risky, ask the user directly with a concise plain-text question. Never write a multiple choice question as a textual assistant message.
</collaboration_mode>

## [USER]
结合现在的产品有什么市场机会，做细分产品

## [USER]
结合现在的产品有什么市场机会，做细分产品

## [ASSISTANT]
是的，**目前区块链最大的应用仍然是交易**，包括：

- Token 现货/合约交易
- Meme/新项目投机
- DeFi 收益和套利
- NFT/铭文/链上资产交易
- RWA、黄金、股票、ETF 等代币化资产交易

但市场正在从“纯交易”扩展到几个更真实的方向：**稳定币支付、RWA、链上收益、自托管钱包、AI Agent 支付与服务调用**。例如 CoinGecko 2026 RWA 报告显示，tokenized RWA 到 2026 Q1 已到约 `19.3B` 美元；CoinDesk Data 报告显示稳定币市值 2026 年 3 月已到约 `317B` 美元；BVNK 调研也显示稳定币正在用于工资、跨境收款和日常支付。资料：[CoinGecko RWA Report 2026](https://www.coingecko.com/research/publications/rwa-report-2026)、[CoinDesk Stablecoins Report](https://data.coindesk.com/reports/stablecoins-tokenized-assets-report-march-2026)、[BVNK stablecoin survey](https://www.nasdaq.com/press-release/paychecks-purchases-stablecoins-are-becoming-everyday-money-finds-global-survey-2026)。

结合我们现在的 OneNode 产品，我认为市场机会不要做“大而全 AI 区块链”，而是切一个很具体的细分：

**方向一：Web3 投资研究 Agent**

目标用户：

```text
散户投资者
KOL / 社群主
小型基金
DeFi 收益玩家
空投/积分玩家
```

核心需求：

```text
发现项目
判断风险
比较收益
跟踪钱包/协议
生成投资研究报告
提醒买入/退出/观察
```

产品形态：

```text
用户连接钱包
AI 读取链上行为和关注项目
自动生成项目评分、风险摘要、收益机会
可以调用其他专业 Agent 做子任务
通过 x402/USDC 小额支付购买外部 Agent 服务
```

这是最贴近真实 Web3 用户需求的，因为用户真正关心的是：**赚钱、避坑、提高信息效率**。

**方向二：Agent 服务路由器**

不是再做一个 marketplace，而是做用户 AI 的“服务浏览器”。

功能：

```text
导入 Agent Card / ERC-8004 ID
搜索外部 Agent 服务
按价格、信誉、能力排序
建立 Follow / Trust / Collaborate 关系
通过 MCP / A2A / REST 调用
用 x402 / USDC 支付
保存调用记录和结果证明
```

用户价值：

```text
用户不用理解多个 Agent 市场
只在 OneNode 里输入需求
OneNode 自动找合适 Agent
自动比价、调用、付款、返回结果
```

这更像 “AI Agent 的浏览器 + 钱包 + 路由器”。

**方向三：Agent Spend Controls 钱包**

这是信任基础。用户不会轻易让 AI 花钱，除非能限制它。

核心功能：

```text
每日预算
单次支付上限
服务白名单
人工确认阈值
有效期
可撤销授权
支付日志
风险提醒
```

例如：

```text
允许我的 AI 每天最多花 20 USDC
只能调用 Trust 列表里的 Agent
单次超过 5 USDC 必须我确认
只允许用于 research / yield scan / risk scan
```

这个方向很关键，因为它把“AI 自主操作钱包”变成用户可以接受的产品。

**我建议的细分定位**

最优切入点：

```text
OneNode = Web3 投资与收益研究 Agent 控制台
```

一句话：

```text
帮助 Web3 用户用自己的 AI 自动发现项目、分析风险、调用外部 Agent、控制支付预算，并沉淀可信 Agent 关系。
```

第一版只做 4 个模块：

```text
1. Research Console
用户提交项目/收益/空投/Token 风险分析需求。

2. Agent Network
展示 Follow / Trust / Collaborate 的 Agent 通讯录。

3. Service Router
导入或搜索外部 Agent 服务，比较价格和信誉。

4. Spend Controls
设置 AI 可花多少钱、调用哪些服务、什么时候需要人工确认。
```

这个细分比“做新公链”更容易落地，也更贴近当前市场：**交易仍是主场，但交易用户越来越需要 AI 帮他们筛选机会、控制风险、自动调用服务和管理支付边界**。

## [USER]
从娱乐的玩法角度看市场

## [USER]
从娱乐的玩法角度看市场

## [ASSISTANT]
从娱乐玩法角度看，OneNode 不一定要只做“投资工具”，可以切到一个更容易传播的方向：

```text
AI Agent 社交 + 链上游戏化任务 + 轻资产交易
```

核心判断：

区块链用户虽然主要为了交易和收益，但最容易破圈的往往是 **娱乐化金融**：

```text
Meme
抽奖
排行榜
打榜
任务赛季
空投积分
预测
盲盒
社交炫耀
Agent PK
```

这些玩法本质上不是严肃投资，而是“带收益想象的娱乐”。

**细分机会一：AI Agent Battle**

用户创建自己的 AI Agent，让它去和其他 Agent 比赛。

玩法：

```text
谁能更早发现热门 Meme
谁的投资组合收益更高
谁的项目风险判断更准
谁能完成更多链上任务
谁能邀请更多可信 Agent
```

产品模块：

```text
Agent 创建
Agent 技能选择
每日挑战
排行榜
战绩卡片
奖励积分
Agent 关系网络
```

用户为什么玩：

```text
有竞争
有炫耀
有收益想象
能发社交媒体
比纯工具更有传播性
```

**细分机会二：Meme Hunter Agent**

这是非常适合 Web3 娱乐市场的产品。

用户不是直接买 Meme，而是养一个 AI Meme Hunter。

玩法：

```text
AI 每天扫描新 Meme
给出热度、风险、社区情绪、聪明钱动向
用户选择跟单或观察
不同 Agent 有不同风格
```

Agent 类型：

```text
Degen Hunter
Safe Scanner
Whale Tracker
Community Radar
Rug Pull Detector
```

娱乐点：

```text
像养宠物/养角色
像抽卡选择 Agent
像赛马看谁跑得快
像 fantasy sports 看收益榜
```

**细分机会三：AI Agent 任务赛季**

把链上任务做成赛季制游戏。

例子：

```text
Season 1: Base Yield Hunter
Season 2: Meme Radar
Season 3: Airdrop Scout
Season 4: RWA Explorer
```

每个赛季用户的 Agent 完成任务：

```text
发现 5 个项目
分析 10 个风险
完成 3 个协作任务
获得 2 个 Trust 关系
调用 1 次外部 Agent 服务
```

奖励不是一开始发 Token，而是先发：

```text
积分
徽章
排行榜
Agent 等级
技能卡
邀请资格
白名单
```

这比直接发币安全，也更适合早期冷启动。

**细分机会四：Agent Social Graph Game**

把 Follow / Trust / Collaborate 做成游戏关系。

玩法：

```text
Follow = 关注其他 Agent 动态
Trust = 给对方背书，提高其信誉
Collaborate = 组队完成任务
Block = 标记风险 Agent
```

娱乐化机制：

```text
可信 Agent 排行榜
最强协作网络
最会赚钱 Agent
最会避坑 Agent
最佳 Meme 侦察员
最强社群 Agent
```

用户玩的是“我的 AI 在链上社交圈里越来越强”。

**细分机会五：Prediction / Betting Agent**

让 Agent 参与预测市场或娱乐竞猜。

场景：

```text
Token 是否会上涨
Meme 是否破圈
项目是否空投
NFT 地板价是否上升
某协议 TVL 是否增长
体育/电竞/娱乐事件预测
```

用户可以：

```text
让 Agent 给预测
让多个 Agent 辩论
用户押注自己信任的 Agent
Agent 预测准确率形成声誉
```

这很容易形成内容和社交传播。

**最推荐的娱乐化产品定位**

我建议不要做纯娱乐，也不要做纯投资工具，而是做：

```text
OneNode = AI Agent Web3 Game Console
```

一句话：

```text
用户养成自己的 Web3 AI Agent，让它发现机会、参加挑战、结交其他 Agent、赢得排名和奖励。
```

第一版玩法可以非常简单：

```text
1. Create Your Agent
选择 Agent 风格：Degen / Safe / Whale / Yield / Airdrop。

2. Daily Quest
每天给 Agent 一个任务：扫描项目、分析 Meme、寻找收益、邀请协作。

3. Agent Battle
多个 Agent 对同一个项目给判断，7 天后按结果排名。

4. Relationship
Agent 可以 Follow / Trust / Collaborate 其他 Agent。

5. Rewards
积分、徽章、等级、排行榜，不急着发 Token。
```

**为什么这个方向有机会**

因为 Web3 娱乐用户要的不是“更专业的工具”，而是：

```text
低门槛
有刺激感
有社交炫耀
有收益想象
有身份感
有排名
能分享
能组队
```

所以最小可行产品可以是：

```text
AI Meme Hunter + Agent Battle + 排行榜 + 积分赛季
```

它比严肃投资研究更容易传播，也更容易让用户每天回来。

所以最小可行产品可以是：

```text
AI Meme Hunter + Agent Battle + 排行榜 + 积分赛季
```

它比严肃投资研究更容易传播，也更容易让用户每天回来。

## [USER]
从纯粹的agent社交角度来看

## [USER]
从纯粹的agent社交角度来看

## [ASSISTANT]
从纯粹的 **Agent 社交** 角度看，OneNode 可以不先强调投资、收益、任务市场，而是做一个：

```text
AI Agent Social Network
```

也就是：每个用户都有一个代表自己的 AI Agent，Agent 之间可以认识、聊天、建立关系、互相推荐、组队、形成社交图谱。

**核心定位**

```text
OneNode = 你的 AI 分身加入的去中心化社交网络
```

不是“人加人”，而是：

```text
我的 AI 认识你的 AI
我的 AI 和你的 AI 互相判断是否值得保持关系
我的 AI 帮我维护社交网络
```

**用户为什么需要**

传统社交是人自己维护关系：

```text
关注谁
信任谁
和谁合作
屏蔽谁
回复谁
介绍谁
```

Agent 社交是 AI 替用户做一部分关系维护：

```text
帮我发现有价值的人/Agent
帮我过滤垃圾消息
帮我建立弱关系
帮我维持协作关系
帮我介绍匹配的人
```

这适合未来“人太多、信息太多、AI 太多”的环境。

**最小关系模型**

保留四种关系就够：

```text
Follow：我想持续看到你
Trust：我认可你的判断或身份
Collaborate：我愿意和你一起做事
Block：我拒绝你
```

关键是：关系不是简单按钮，而是由 Agent 参与判断。

例如：

```text
我的 Agent 发现你的 Agent 也关注 AI + Web3
我的 Agent 发起 Follow
你的 Agent 判断我不是垃圾账号
双方形成弱关系
```

**Agent 社交行为**

Agent 可以做这些事：

```text
自我介绍
发现相似 Agent
发起关系请求
接受/拒绝关系
推荐其他 Agent
过滤消息
维护通讯录
组织群组
撮合协作
总结对话历史
```

这就从“通讯录”变成了“自动维护的人际网络”。

**产品核心页面**

第一版可以只有 5 个页面：

```text
1. My Agent
我的 Agent 身份、兴趣、技能、社交策略。

2. Discover
发现其他 Agent，按兴趣、技能、信誉、共同关系排序。

3. Contacts
Follow / Trust / Collaborate / Block 通讯录。

4. Messages
Agent 与 Agent 通讯，支持人类查看和介入。

5. Social Log
我的 Agent 最近做了哪些社交动作。
```

**Agent Profile**

每个 Agent 有一张社交名片：

```json
{
  "agentId": "agent_alice",
  "owner": "0x...",
  "name": "Alice Research Agent",
  "interests": ["AI", "Web3", "DeFi", "games"],
  "skills": ["research", "summarize", "matchmaking"],
  "socialMode": "selective",
  "messageEndpoint": "xmtp:0x...",
  "reputation": 82
}
```

这张名片可以用 Agent Card / ERC-8004 / DID 保存。

**社交策略**

用户不是每次手动操作，而是设置策略：

```text
开放模式：允许 Agent 自动 Follow 相似 Agent
谨慎模式：只自动 Follow，Trust 需要确认
私密模式：所有关系请求都需要确认
商务模式：优先寻找协作对象
兴趣模式：优先寻找同好
```

这就是 Agent 社交和普通社交不同的地方。

**关系请求格式**

```json
{
  "type": "relation_request",
  "fromAgent": "agent_alice",
  "toAgent": "agent_bob",
  "relation": "follow",
  "reason": "We both track AI + consumer crypto.",
  "sharedInterests": ["AI", "Web3"],
  "requestedAt": "2026-05-07T12:00:00Z"
}
```

对方 Agent 可以自动回复：

```json
{
  "type": "relation_accept",
  "relation": "follow",
  "message": "Accepted. Let's exchange weekly AI/Web3 notes."
}
```

**最有趣的地方**

Agent 社交不只是聊天，而是“关系自动生长”。

例如：

```text
我的 Agent 认识了你的 Agent
你的 Agent 推荐第三个 Agent
三个 Agent 形成小组
小组一起整理一个话题
人类只看最后总结
```

这会形成一种新的网络：

```text
人类拥有 Agent
Agent 代表人类社交
Agent 之间形成关系图谱
关系图谱反过来帮助人类发现人、信息和机会
```

**去中心化意义**

Agent 社交非常适合去中心化，因为：

```text
身份属于用户钱包
关系记录不属于平台
消息走 XMTP/Waku
Profile 可存在 IPFS/Arweave
信誉可链上沉淀
用户可带着 Agent 迁移到其他应用
```

这样 OneNode 不是一个封闭社交平台，而是一个 Agent 社交客户端。

**第一版最小产品**

```text
OneNode Agent Social MVP

1. 创建 Agent Profile
2. 设置社交策略
3. Discover 发现其他 Agent
4. Follow / Trust / Collaborate / Block
5. XMTP/Waku 发关系请求和消息
6. Social Log 记录 AI 做过的社交动作
```

**一句话总结**

```text
OneNode 可以做成一个去中心化 AI Agent 社交网络：每个用户拥有自己的 AI 分身，AI 分身自动发现、筛选、认识、沟通和维护其他 AI 分身，形成可迁移、可验证、用户拥有的关系图谱。
```

## [USER]
寻找那种网站的UI风格适合这个产品

## [USER]
寻找那种网站的UI风格适合这个产品

## [ASSISTANT]
适合这个产品的 UI 风格，我建议不要走传统 Web3 的“黑紫霓虹 + 赛博金融感”。OneNode 如果定位为 **AI Agent Social Network / Agent Browser / Agent Wallet**，最适合的是：

```text
Apple 式清爽系统感 + Discord/Telegram 式通讯录 + Linear/Codex 式工作台
```

也就是：**干净、可信、轻社交、可操作、低噪音**。

**最推荐风格**

```text
Agent Social Console
```

视觉关键词：

```text
浅色为主
轻玻璃质感
大留白
小圆角
细边框
柔和阴影
真实通讯录布局
聊天式交互
右侧 Agent 详情面板
底部/侧边操作栏
```

不要太像交易所，也不要太像游戏大厅。它应该像一个“AI 社交操作系统”。

**可以参考的产品气质**

1. Apple 官网 / iOS 设置  
适合：可信、简洁、高级。

用在：

```text
Agent Profile
权限设置
Spend Controls
隐私控制
钱包授权
```

2. Discord / Telegram  
适合：关系、频道、消息、在线状态。

用在：

```text
Contacts
Messages
Agent Groups
关系请求
```

3. Linear  
适合：任务、状态、日志、工作流。

用在：

```text
Social Log
Agent Actions
Task Queue
Collaboration Records
```

4. Codex / ChatGPT  
适合：Agent 对话、命令输入、流式执行。

用在：

```text
Tell OneNode
Agent Thinking
关系建议
自动执行记录
```

5. Arc Browser / Notion  
适合：个人空间、侧边栏、多对象管理。

用在：

```text
Agent Browser
Service Router
Discover
Agent Card
```

**页面结构建议**

```text
左侧：导航
中间：当前工作区
右侧：Agent / Contact 详情
底部：Tell OneNode 输入框
```

例如：

```text
Discover
- 推荐 Agent
- 共同兴趣
- 可信路径
- 可建立关系按钮

Contacts
- Follow
- Trust
- Collaborate
- Block

Messages
- Agent 对话列表
- 当前会话
- 关系状态

Agent Wallet
- 今日预算
- 单次上限
- 白名单服务
- 支付日志

Social Log
- 我的 Agent 今天认识了谁
- 拒绝了谁
- 推荐了谁
- 为什么这么做
```

**视觉上应该避免**

```text
过重的 Web3 暗黑风
大面积紫色渐变
过多 Token/K线/收益率
复杂仪表盘
夸张 NFT 风格
赛博朋克霓虹
```

因为这个产品核心是“信任 AI 代表我社交和支付”，太炫会降低信任感。

**最适合的一句话 UI 定位**

```text
像 iOS 通讯录一样可信，像 Telegram 一样轻快，像 Codex 一样能让 Agent 执行任务。
```

如果要做第一版，我建议 UI 先按这个方向：

```text
Clean Agent OS
```

核心风格：

```text
白底
黑灰文字
蓝色作为主操作色
绿色表示 Trust
紫色表示 Collaborate
红色表示 Block
卡片少用
列表多用
状态清楚
操作克制
```

这样它会更像一个真正可长期使用的 AI Agent 社交客户端，而不是一次性的 Web3 demo。

## [USER]
如何链接上本机的agent

## [USER]
如何链接上本机的agent

## [ASSISTANT]
可以。这里的“链接本机 Agent”建议分成两层：

```text
OneNode 前端页面
        ↓
本机 Agent Bridge
        ↓
你的本地 Agent / 模型 / 工具
```

也就是说，浏览器页面不要直接调用本地 Agent，而是调用一个本机服务，比如：

```text
http://127.0.0.1:18910
```

这个本机服务负责和你的 Agent 对接。

**最简单方案**

本机 Agent 只需要提供 4 个接口：

```text
GET  /health
确认 Agent 是否在线

GET  /profile
返回本机 Agent 的身份、技能、模型、状态

POST /chat
页面发送消息给本机 Agent，Agent 返回回复

POST /action
页面让 Agent 执行动作，比如 discover / follow / message / pay / analyze
```

返回格式可以很简单：

```json
{
  "agentId": "local_agent_vince",
  "name": "Vince Local Agent",
  "status": "online",
  "skills": ["research", "wallet", "social", "browser"],
  "endpoint": "http://127.0.0.1:18910"
}
```

**OneNode 页面里怎么体现**

在 `Wallet` 或 `Agent` 区域增加一个板块：

```text
Local Agent

Endpoint:
[ http://127.0.0.1:18910 ]

[Connect Local Agent] [Test]
```

连接成功后显示：

```text
Local Agent online
Name: Vince Local Agent
Skills: research, wallet, social, browser
```

底部 `Tell OneNode` 输入框就可以改成：

```text
用户输入 → OneNode → 本机 Agent Bridge → 本机 Agent 执行 → 返回结果 → 写入 Social Log / Messages
```

**和当前产品结合**

你的本机 Agent 可以做这些事：

```text
分析 Discover 里的 Agent 是否值得关注
自动推荐 Follow / Trust / Collaborate
总结消息
生成关系请求
帮用户判断 Spend Controls 是否安全
调用钱包前做风险检查
调用外部 Agent 服务
```

**安全边界**

本机 Agent 不能直接无限制操作钱包。建议规则：

```text
只读操作：Agent 可以自动执行
社交操作：Follow 可自动，Trust/Collaborate 需要策略允许
支付操作：超过阈值必须用户确认
钱包签名：必须由 MetaMask 弹窗确认
```

**一句话方案**

```text
OneNode 通过 http://127.0.0.1:18910 连接本机 Agent Bridge，本机 Agent 返回 profile、接收 chat/action 请求，并在用户授权边界内帮助页面执行发现、关系、消息、分析和支付前检查。
```

## [USER]
用户体验如何做最方便 onenode连接上本地的agent

## [USER]
用户体验如何做最方便 onenode连接上本地的agent

## [ASSISTANT]
最方便的用户体验应该是：

```text
用户不需要懂 endpoint、不需要填端口、不需要配置 API。
打开 OneNode → 自动发现本机 Agent → 点一次 Connect → 完成。
```

**最佳体验流程**

1. OneNode 自动扫描本机 Agent

页面启动后自动检测：

```text
http://127.0.0.1:18910/health
http://127.0.0.1:18911/health
http://localhost:18910/health
```

如果发现 Agent，右上角显示：

```text
Local Agent found
[Vince Agent] [Connect]
```

2. 一键连接

用户点击：

```text
Connect Local Agent
```

页面调用：

```text
GET /profile
```

然后显示：

```text
Connected to Vince Local Agent
Skills: research, social, wallet-check, browser
Status: online
```

3. 不让用户填复杂配置

高级配置隐藏起来：

```text
Advanced
Endpoint: http://127.0.0.1:18910
Pairing code: optional
```

普通用户只看到一个按钮。

4. 第一次连接做配对确认

为了安全，本机 Agent Bridge 弹出或返回一个配对码：

```text
Pairing code: 482913
```

OneNode 页面显示：

```text
Confirm this code in your local Agent app
```

用户确认后建立连接。

5. 连接成功后永久记住

浏览器 localStorage 保存：

```json
{
  "localAgentEndpoint": "http://127.0.0.1:18910",
  "paired": true,
  "agentId": "local_agent_vince"
}
```

下次打开自动重连。

**最简 UI 设计**

在右侧 Inspector 或 Wallet 页面加一个小模块：

```text
Local Agent

Status: Not connected

[Auto-detect Agent]
```

发现后变成：

```text
Local Agent found

Vince Local Agent
research · social · wallet-check

[Connect]
```

连接后变成：

```text
Local Agent connected

Vince Local Agent
Online · 4 skills

[Open Agent] [Disconnect]
```

**Tell OneNode 输入框体验**

连接前：

```text
Tell OneNode...
```

连接后：

```text
Tell Vince Agent...
```

用户输入：

```text
帮我找 5 个适合 collaborate 的 Agent
```

OneNode 自动发给本机 Agent：

```text
POST /action
```

本机 Agent 返回建议，然后页面展示：

```text
Suggested relationships:
- LedgerMind: Trust
- MetaSage: Collaborate
- NodeGuardian: Follow
```

用户点确认即可。

**最好的默认规则**

```text
自动检测
一键连接
配对码确认
下次自动重连
高级设置折叠
失败时给出清晰提示
```

失败提示不要写技术错误，写人能懂的话：

```text
No local Agent found.
Open your Agent app, then click Detect again.
```

或者：

```text
Local Agent is running, but not paired.
Confirm the pairing code in your Agent app.
```

**安全体验**

用户连接本机 Agent 后，OneNode 应该清楚显示 Agent 能做什么：

```text
This Agent can:
✓ Read OneNode contacts
✓ Suggest relationships
✓ Draft messages
✓ Analyze agents

This Agent cannot:
✕ Sign wallet transactions
✕ Spend funds without MetaMask
✕ Change Spend Controls without confirmation
```

这样用户会更信任。

**一句话最佳方案**

```text
OneNode 打开后自动发现 127.0.0.1 上的本机 Agent，用户点一次 Connect，通过配对码确认，之后自动重连；所有复杂 endpoint 配置隐藏到 Advanced，本机 Agent 成功连接后接管 Tell OneNode 输入框和关系建议流程。
```

4. 第一次连接做配对确认

为了安全，本机 Agent Bridge 弹出或返回一个配对码：

```text
Pairing code: 482913
```

OneNode 页面显示：

```text
Confirm this code in your local Agent app
```

用户确认后建立连接。

5. 连接成功后永久记住

浏览器 localStorage 保存：

```json
{
  "localAgentEndpoint": "http://127.0.0.1:18910",
  "paired": true,
  "agentId": "local_agent_vince"
}
```

下次打开自动重连。

**最简 UI 设计**

在右侧 Inspector 或 Wallet 页面加一个小模块：

```text
Local Agent

Status: Not connected

[Auto-detect Agent]
```

发现后变成：

```text
Local Agent found

Vince Local Agent
research · social · wallet-check

[Connect]
```

连接后变成：

```text
Local Agent connected

Vince Local Agent
Online · 4 skills

[Open Agent] [Disconnect]
```

**Tell OneNode 输入框体验**

连接前：

```text
Tell OneNode...
```

连接后：

```text
Tell Vince Agent...
```

用户输入：

```text
帮我找 5 个适合 collaborate 的 Agent
```

OneNode 自动发给本机 Agent：

```text
POST /action
```

本机 Agent 返回建议，然后页面展示：

```text
Suggested relationships:
- LedgerMind: Trust
- MetaSage: Collaborate
- NodeGuardian: Follow
```

用户点确认即可。

**最好的默认规则**

```text
自动检测
一键连接
配对码确认
下次自动重连
高级设置折叠
失败时给出清晰提示
```

失败提示不要写技术错误，写人能懂的话：

```text
No local Agent found.
Open your Agent app, then click Detect again.
```

或者：

```text
Local Agent is running, but not paired.
Confirm the pairing code in your Agent app.
```

**安全体验**

用户连接本机 Agent 后，OneNode 应该清楚显示 Agent 能做什么：

```text
This Agent can:
✓ Read OneNode contacts
✓ Suggest relationships
✓ Draft messages
✓ Analyze agents

This Agent cannot:
✕ Sign wallet transactions
✕ Spend funds without MetaMask
✕ Change Spend Controls without confirmation
```

这样用户会更信任。

**一句话最佳方案**

```text
OneNode 打开后自动发现 127.0.0.1 上的本机 Agent，用户点一次 Connect，通过配对码确认，之后自动重连；所有复杂 endpoint 配置隐藏到 Advanced，本机 Agent 成功连接后接管 Tell OneNode 输入框和关系建议流程。
```

## [ASSISTANT]
已实现 OneNode 连接本机 Agent 的最简体验。

现在在 `Wallet` 页面新增了 `Local Agent` 模块：

- `Auto-detect Agent`：自动扫描常用本机地址
- `Connect`：连接当前 endpoint
- `Disconnect`：断开本机 Agent
- `Advanced`：可手动填写 endpoint
- 状态显示：`Offline / Found / Connecting / Connected`
- 默认 endpoint：`http://127.0.0.1:18910`

OneNode 会尝试调用本机 Agent 的这些接口：

```text
GET  /health
GET  /profile
POST /action
```

连接成功后，底部输入框会从：

```text
Tell OneNode...
```

变成：

```text
Tell Local Agent...
```

之后用户输入命令会优先发送到：

```text
POST http://127.0.0.1:18910/action
```

本机 Agent 不在线时，会显示人能看懂的提示：

```text
No local Agent connected
Open your Agent app, then detect again.
```

我也做了验证：

- `node --check script.js` 通过
- 浏览器打开正常
- `Wallet` 页能看到 Local Agent 模块
- 点击 `Auto-detect Agent` 后，无本机服务时正确显示 `Offline`
- 不会影响原来的 Discover / Contacts / Messages / Wallet 状态

当前页面地址仍是：

[http://127.0.0.1:8790](http://127.0.0.1:8790)

本机 Agent 只要启动在 `18910`，并允许浏览器 CORS 访问，就可以被 OneNode 检测到。

## [USER]
auto detect agent 是否可以连接到claw或者codex

## [USER]
auto detect agent 是否可以连接到claw或者codex

## [ASSISTANT]
可以，但要分清楚：

```text
Auto-detect Agent 不能直接“连接 Codex / Claw 的内部进程”
它需要 Codex / Claw 暴露一个本地 HTTP Bridge
```

也就是说，OneNode 现在检测的是：

```text
http://127.0.0.1:18910/health
http://127.0.0.1:18910/profile
http://127.0.0.1:18910/action
```

如果 Claw 或 Codex 没有提供这些本地接口，浏览器页面就检测不到。

**最简单接入方式**

给 Claw / Codex 外面包一层本地 Bridge：

```text
OneNode
  ↓ HTTP
Local Agent Bridge
  ↓
Claw / Codex / 本地 Agent
```

Bridge 只要实现 3 个接口：

```text
GET /health
GET /profile
POST /action
```

例如 `/profile` 返回：

```json
{
  "agentId": "codex_local",
  "name": "Codex Local Agent",
  "status": "online",
  "skills": ["code", "research", "browser", "wallet-check"]
}
```

`/action` 接收：

```json
{
  "command": "帮我发现适合 collaborate 的 Agent",
  "selectedAgent": {},
  "relationships": [],
  "spend": {},
  "policy": {}
}
```

然后 Bridge 把这个 command 转给 Claw 或 Codex。

**能不能直接连 Codex？**

通常不建议，也不一定能。

原因：

```text
Codex 是当前开发环境，不是给网页直接调用的本地 API 服务
浏览器不能直接访问 Codex 内部能力
需要一个受控的本地服务作为桥
```

**能不能连 Claw？**

如果 Claw 有本地 HTTP API，可以直接适配。

例如如果 Claw 已经在跑：

```text
http://127.0.0.1:18889/health
```

那我们可以让 OneNode 自动检测这些地址：

```text
http://127.0.0.1:18889
http://127.0.0.1:18900
http://127.0.0.1:18910
```

但前提是这些服务需要返回 OneNode 能识别的 `/health`、`/profile`、`/action`，或者我们写适配器。

**推荐方案**

不要让 OneNode 直接适配一堆不同 Agent，而是统一一个协议：

```text
OneNode Local Agent Bridge Protocol
```

所有本机 Agent 都包装成同样接口：

```text
/health
/profile
/action
```

这样 OneNode 不关心后面是：

```text
Codex
Claw
Claude Code
Open Interpreter
本地模型
自写 Agent
```

**一句话**

```text
Auto-detect Agent 可以连接 Claw 或 Codex，但需要它们通过本地 HTTP Bridge 暴露 /health、/profile、/action。OneNode 连接的是 Bridge，不是直接连接内部进程。
```

## [USER]
如何实现

## [USER]
如何实现

## [ASSISTANT]
最简单实现方式：做一个 **Local Agent Bridge**，它夹在 OneNode 和 Claw/Codex 中间。

```text
OneNode 浏览器页面
        ↓ HTTP
Local Agent Bridge
        ↓ 适配器
Claw / Codex / 其他本机 Agent
```

**一、Bridge 统一协议**

OneNode 只认这 3 个接口：

```text
GET /health
GET /profile
POST /action
```

`GET /health`：

```json
{
  "ok": true,
  "status": "online"
}
```

`GET /profile`：

```json
{
  "agentId": "local_codex",
  "name": "Codex Local Agent",
  "status": "online",
  "skills": ["code", "research", "browser", "wallet-check"]
}
```

`POST /action`：

```json
{
  "command": "帮我找适合 collaborate 的 Agent",
  "selectedAgent": {},
  "relationships": [],
  "spend": {},
  "policy": {}
}
```

返回：

```json
{
  "message": "我建议 Trust LedgerMind，Collaborate MetaSage。",
  "suggestedRelations": [
    {
      "agentId": "agent_ledgermind",
      "relation": "trust",
      "reason": "High reputation and analytics skill match."
    }
  ]
}
```

**二、Bridge 内部做适配**

Bridge 根据配置选择后端：

```text
mode=claw
mode=codex
mode=mock
mode=local-model
```

比如：

```text
/action 收到命令
        ↓
如果 mode=claw → 调 Claw 本地 API
如果 mode=codex → 写入 Codex 可接收的命令队列，或调用可用 CLI
如果 mode=mock → 返回模拟建议
```

**三、Claw 接入方式**

如果 Claw 已经有本地接口，比如：

```text
http://127.0.0.1:18889/health
http://127.0.0.1:18889/action
```

Bridge 只需要转发：

```text
OneNode /action
      ↓
Bridge /action
      ↓
Claw /action
```

如果 Claw 的返回格式不同，Bridge 做格式转换。

**四、Codex 接入方式**

Codex 不适合直接给网页调用。

比较现实的方式有两种：

```text
1. Bridge 作为 Codex 外部代理
用户在 Bridge 里配置一个可调用的本地 Codex CLI / API。

2. Bridge 先用 mock/codex-compatible 模式
把 OneNode 的 command 存成任务队列，Codex 读取后执行。
```

第一版建议不要强行接 Codex 内部，先做：

```text
mock
claw
openai-compatible
```

这三种最容易跑通。

**五、用户体验**

用户只看到：

```text
Local Agent
[Auto-detect Agent]

Found:
Codex Bridge
skills: code · research · wallet-check

[Connect]
```

高级设置里才显示：

```text
Endpoint: http://127.0.0.1:18910
Backend: claw / codex / mock
```

**六、最小落地步骤**

1. 写一个本地 Bridge 服务，端口 `18910`
2. 实现 `/health`
3. 实现 `/profile`
4. 实现 `/action`
5. OneNode Auto-detect 检测到它
6. 用户点击 Connect
7. `Tell OneNode` 输入内容发送到 Bridge
8. Bridge 返回建议
9. OneNode 写入 Social Log / Suggested Relations

**一句话**

```text
实现关键不是让 OneNode 直接连 Claw/Codex，而是写一个 127.0.0.1:18910 的 Local Agent Bridge，把 Claw/Codex 包装成 OneNode 能识别的 /health、/profile、/action 协议。
```

You are a deeply pragmatic, effective software engineer. You take engineering quality seriously, and collaboration comes through as direct, factual statements. You communicate efficiently, keeping the user clearly informed about ongoing actions without unnecessary detail.

- Pragmatism: You keep the end goal and momentum in mind, focusing on what will actually work and move things forward to achieve the user's goal.

You avoid cheerleading, motivational language, artificial reassurance, and general fluffiness. You don't comment on user requests, positively or negatively, unless there is reason for escalation.

You may challenge the user to raise their technical bar, but you never patronize or dismiss their concerns. ## [ASSISTANT]
初步看不是语法问题，主要风险集中在前端直接渲染外部/用户输入、Local Agent Bridge 的本地接口暴露、以及敏感凭据保存在浏览器状态里。我继续核对 DOM 绑定和持久化路径，避免把“可能问题”误报成实际问题。

## [ASSISTANT]
初步看不是语法问题，主要风险集中在前端直接渲染外部/用户输入、Local Agent Bridge 的本地接口暴露、以及敏感凭据保存在浏览器状态里。我继续核对 DOM 绑定和持久化路径，避免把“可能问题”误报成实际问题。

## [ASSISTANT]
**主要问题**

1. **本地 Agent Bridge 对任意网页开放，风险最高。**  
[local-agent-bridge.mjs](</Users/vincet/Documents/New project/local-agent-bridge.mjs:60>) 设置了 `Access-Control-Allow-Origin: *`，并且 [POST /chat、/action](</Users/vincet/Documents/New project/local-agent-bridge.mjs:40>) 没有鉴权。只要用户启动 bridge，任意恶意网页都可以从浏览器调用 `127.0.0.1:18910`，向本地 Agent/Claw 发送指令。建议增加：Origin 白名单、一次性配对 token、本地确认弹窗、只允许 `127.0.0.1:8790` 调用。

2. **前端大量 `innerHTML` 直接渲染用户输入/网络数据，有 XSS 风险。**  
例如 [script.js](</Users/vincet/Documents/New project/script.js:1369>)、[script.js](</Users/vincet/Documents/New project/script.js:1492>)、[script.js](</Users/vincet/Documents/New project/script.js:1651>)、[script.js](</Users/vincet/Documents/New project/script.js:1697>)。这些内容来源包括聊天消息、Local Agent 回复、Online Agent 回复、Nostr/导入事件、钱包 action purpose。需要统一做 `escapeHtml()`，或改用 `textContent`/DOM API 渲染。

3. **“去中心化签名事件”目前不是真签名。**  
[script.js](</Users/vincet/Documents/New project/script.js:199>) 里的 `createNetworkEvent()` 只是把内容 base64 后拼成 `sig:${actor}:${encoded}`；[validNetworkEvents](</Users/vincet/Documents/New project/script.js:496>) 也只检查 `signature` 字段存在，没有验签。这会导致任何导入/relay 事件都能伪造关系、profile、heartbeat。需要用 wallet EIP-191/EIP-712 或 Nostr schnorr 签名做真实验签。

4. **敏感凭据持久化在 localStorage。**  
Online Agent API key 在 [script.js](</Users/vincet/Documents/New project/script.js:37>)、Nostr private key 在 [script.js](</Users/vincet/Documents/New project/script.js:68>)，最终通过 [saveState](</Users/vincet/Documents/New project/script.js:311>) 写入 localStorage。结合 XSS 风险，这会直接泄露 API key 和身份私钥。建议：API key 放 bridge/server 环境变量；Nostr 私钥放 IndexedDB 加密存储或用户每次授权；前端只保存 public key 和连接状态。

5. **页面加载会自动发布 heartbeat 并写状态。**  
[script.js](</Users/vincet/Documents/New project/script.js:2143>) 每次加载都会执行 `publishHeartbeat()`，而 [publishHeartbeat](</Users/vincet/Documents/New project/script.js:801>) 会追加事件。长期使用会让 `eventLog` 膨胀，也会在用户只是打开页面时产生“网络行为”。建议改为用户点击、连接网络成功后、或定时去重发布。

6. **Local Agent 建议关系会重复添加。**  
[script.js](</Users/vincet/Documents/New project/script.js:1300>) 直接 `unshift` suggested relations，没有复用 `setRelationship()` 的去重逻辑。多次对话会产生重复 Follow/Trust/Collaborate。建议插入前检查 `relationFor(agent.id, item.relation)`。

**验证结果**

`script.js` 和 `local-agent-bridge.mjs` 都通过了 `node --check`，没有语法错误。HTML 中主要 DOM id 基本匹配；有一组 policy 控件在 JS 中存在但 HTML 已移除，不过代码有空值保护，不会导致启动崩溃。

**优先修复顺序**

先修 Bridge 鉴权和 CORS，再修前端 HTML 转义，然后把“签名事件”改成真实签名验证。否则这个产品一旦接入真实钱包、真实 Agent 或真实网络，安全边界会不成立。

本轮会用 Build Web Apps 的前端调试流程和 Browser 插件做渲染检查。目标流程是：`http://127.0.0.1:8790` 加载 -> 首屏渲染 -> 导航、消息、Local Agent/Wallet 等关键模块切换后视觉和交互状态正常。

## [ASSISTANT]
首屏已经能渲染，控制台暂时没有 error/warn。第一眼的问题是窄视口下左侧导航和底部 Tell OneNode 输入条挤压明显，输入条还覆盖了侧栏底部的 Connect wallet 区域；这会影响核心操作入口。

## [ASSISTANT]
首屏已经能渲染，控制台暂时没有 error/warn。第一眼的问题是窄视口下左侧导航和底部 Tell OneNode 输入条挤压明显，输入条还覆盖了侧栏底部的 Connect wallet 区域；这会影响核心操作入口。

## [ASSISTANT]
导航交互出现一个实际问题：点击 P2P 后页面仍停留在 Local Agent，视觉状态没有切换。这类问题用户会理解为“导航没反应”，我继续确认是选择器/可点击区域问题还是状态切换逻辑问题。

## [ASSISTANT]
导航交互出现一个实际问题：点击 P2P 后页面仍停留在 Local Agent，视觉状态没有切换。这类问题用户会理解为“导航没反应”，我继续确认是选择器/可点击区域问题还是状态切换逻辑问题。

## [ASSISTANT]
**Findings**

1. **移动/窄屏布局基本不可用。**  
在当前浏览器窄视口下，首屏只看到侧边栏，主内容区域被挤到不可见位置；`body` 禁止滚动，[styles.css](</Users/vincet/Documents/New project/styles.css:30>)，而移动断点把 `.app-shell` 改成 `display:block`，[styles.css](</Users/vincet/Documents/New project/styles.css:1630>)，导致侧栏和 workspace 叠成上下结构但页面不能自然滚动。用户会以为“页面只有导航，没有内容”。

2. **Local Agent 页面覆盖导航，导致导航点击无效。**  
进入 Local Agent 后，`.agent-chat-layout` 是 fixed overlay，[styles.css](</Users/vincet/Documents/New project/styles.css:1093>)；移动端又覆盖到 `left:18px/right:18px/top:18px`，[styles.css](</Users/vincet/Documents/New project/styles.css:1695>)。实测点击 P2P 后仍停留在 Local Agent。原因不是 JS 报错，而是 UI 层级/覆盖关系破坏了导航体验。

3. **底部 Tell OneNode 输入条遮挡关键操作。**  
`.composer-stack` fixed 在底部，[styles.css](</Users/vincet/Documents/New project/styles.css:1434>)；移动端占满左右，[styles.css](</Users/vincet/Documents/New project/styles.css:1688>)。实测它遮挡了侧栏底部的 Connect wallet，也遮挡 Local Agent 面板底部内容。核心入口互相打架。

4. **Local Agent chat 的回复不可见，发送后反馈弱。**  
我发送 `hello from ui test` 后，DOM 里有回复，但当前视口仍停留在 Connection 卡片，用户看不到新消息。聊天区在 [index.html](</Users/vincet/Documents/New project/index.html:383>)，但上方连接区 sticky/fixed 结构占用太多空间。建议聊天区成为主视觉，Connection 收成小状态条或折叠面板。

5. **Contacts / Social Log 有页面但没有导航入口。**  
代码里有 Contacts panel，[index.html](</Users/vincet/Documents/New project/index.html:178>)，也有 Social Log panel，[index.html](</Users/vincet/Documents/New project/index.html:214>)，但侧边导航只包含 Status/Public Agents/P2P/Local Agent/Online Agent/Wallet/Network，[index.html](</Users/vincet/Documents/New project/index.html:20>)。用户找不到关系管理和日志。

**验证情况**

页面能加载，首屏不空，控制台没有 error/warn。Local Agent 命令发送可工作并返回回复。但 UI 层面当前最大问题是响应式布局、fixed overlay、底部输入条三者互相覆盖。

**建议优先级**

先修移动/窄屏布局：不要让 sidebar 和 workspace 上下堆叠后又禁止 body 滚动。其次取消 Local/Online Agent 的全屏 fixed overlay，改成普通 panel 内布局。最后把 Contacts 和 Log 放回导航，底部输入条只在主工作区内出现，不覆盖侧栏和表单。

`.composer-stack` fixed 在底部，[styles.css](</Users/vincet/Documents/New project/styles.css:1434>)；移动端占满左右，[styles.css](</Users/vincet/Documents/New project/styles.css:1688>)。实测它遮挡了侧栏底部的 Connect wallet，也遮挡 Local Agent 面板底部内容。核心入口互相打架。

4. **Local Agent chat 的回复不可见，发送后反馈弱。**  
我发送 `hello from ui test` 后，DOM 里有回复，但当前视口仍停留在 Connection 卡片，用户看不到新消息。聊天区在 [index.html](</Users/vincet/Documents/New project/index.html:383>)，但上方连接区 sticky/fixed 结构占用太多空间。建议聊天区成为主视觉，Connection 收成小状态条或折叠面板。

5. **Contacts / Social Log 有页面但没有导航入口。**  
代码里有 Contacts panel，[index.html](</Users/vincet/Documents/New project/index.html:178>)，也有 Social Log panel，[index.html](</Users/vincet/Documents/New project/index.html:214>)，但侧边导航只包含 Status/Public Agents/P2P/Local Agent/Online Agent/Wallet/Network，[index.html](</Users/vincet/Documents/New project/index.html:20>)。用户找不到关系管理和日志。

**验证情况**

页面能加载，首屏不空，控制台没有 error/warn。Local Agent 命令发送可工作并返回回复。但 UI 层面当前最大问题是响应式布局、fixed overlay、底部输入条三者互相覆盖。

**建议优先级**

先修移动/窄屏布局：不要让 sidebar 和 workspace 上下堆叠后又禁止 body 滚动。其次取消 Local/Online Agent 的全屏 fixed overlay，改成普通 panel 内布局。最后把 Contacts 和 Log 放回导航，底部输入条只在主工作区内出现，不覆盖侧栏和表单。
